NEW EXACT ALL-HEIGHT ALL-k ORIGINAL SIGNED FJ330 24 SOURCE SUBPACKET EXCLUSION: Under original primitive Gaussian split prime norm p²=a²+b²,q²=c²+d² with distinct p<q≡1mod8 primes, all signed/swap orientations normalize to canonical a,c positive odd,b,d positive even. For ODD-U original signed 24 FJ quadratic free third Y, let X=ac (odd),Y0=bd (even), (U,Z0)=(X+Y0,X−Y0) or (X−Y0,X+Y0). Original integer third-root discriminant Δ_Y/4=(pq U)²−Z0⁴ must be a NONNEGATIVE INTEGER SQUARE. For each canonical orientation, p∤Z0 and q∤Z0: if p|ac∓bd then q²=c²+d²≡(a²+b²)d²/a²≡0modp, contradict p≠q; likewise q by symmetry. X,Y0 opposite parity ⇒gcd(U,Z0)=gcd(X,Y0)=gcd(ac,bd). Hence gcd(N,Z0)=gcd(ac,bd) for N=pq|U| odd.
FOURTH-POWER PRIMITIVITY LEMMA: If N,Z0 odd with N>1, gcd(N,Z0)=1 and D=N²−Z0⁴=H²≥0 integer, then H even, gcd(N,H)=1, positive odd N±H are coprime and product Z0⁴ ⇒each is an odd fourth power u⁴,v⁴. Therefore 2N=u⁴+v⁴≡2mod16, forcing N≡1mod8. (H=0 would require N=Z0² and gcd1 implies N1, impossible.) Since pq≡1mod8, for EVERY original signed 24 ODD-U class with gcd(ac,bd)=1 and |U|≡7mod8, N≡7mod8 CONTRADICTION. Hence this exact primitive/angular subpacket admits NO RATIONAL THIRD Y at ANY p,q,r or k. This is independent of physical third norm-prime necessity; stronger than local 2-adic square test. Does NOT globally empty all24 source keys or yield unrestricted #S_B(delta)=o(B^(1/3)). The A11 globally-empty16 were RETRACTED by A12 and stay retracted; counts potentially nonempty130, genuinely globally & balanced uncounted128=72 square+6 linear+50 quartic; GLOBAL_CREDIT0 unchanged.
EXACT FINITE INDEPENDENT PROOF CONTROL (NOT NEW GLOBAL PRIME HORIZON): p≡1mod8 split prime≤20000, all p<q<2p split:134173 first-two prime pairs;23597 with gcd(ac,bd)=1; two ODD-U canonical signed pair-class tests per pair yielded23652 all-height forbidden classes (|U|≡7mod8), 14423 have D≥0. ## Stage37 229ED — uniform Mordell-Lang + Gaussian two-descent rank firewall
STATUS=PROVED_MOVING_LOW_INNER_SLOPE_POPULATION_POWER_SAVING; COMPLEMENT_HIGH_SLOPE_OPEN; GLOBAL_COUNTING_CREDIT=0
CARD_DOC_ID=1Vc7NU_MdM1OrhX5ND5BE2a7JsUdpF04ocrXNVvM0ti4

Consequently max(E,Y)<=M gives #C_EY(Q)<=M^o(1), summing O(M) primitive Pyth slopes gives #original FULL physical points Oeps(M^(1+eps)) uniformly in B; M=B^(1/3-sigma) gives O_sigma(B^(1/3-sigma/2)). This OVERCOMES earlier Faltings fixed-M nonuniform constant, but only LOW inner slopes, not all-high count.

No count from a=gcd(e,y)<=B/W by itself; no automatic density gain by combining with229DZ/EA. 229EC rank E4>=1 DOES NOT block the rank-sensitive uniform Mordell-Lang theorem, which works for arbitrary rank. ## Stage37-229EC — uniform rank-zero inner-face strategy DISPROVED, exact positive-lift firewall
STATUS=PROVED_UNIFORM_STRUCTURAL_NEGATIVE_FOR_RANK_ZERO_ONLY; NOT_NEGATIVE_FOR_RANK_LT_FIVE; GLOBAL_CREDIT=0
CARD_DOC_ID=1GhTrCf9PEFw-A5wM24ZD63FOOB8BEcXQ9Na3RhdDVLw
For every primitive positive E²+Y²=W², the E4 factor from229EB, after X=s+W², is T²=X(X-E²)(X-Y²). P0=(W²,EYW) has X(2P0)=((E⁴+E²Y²+Y⁴)/(2EYW))², nonintegral by primitive coprimality and parity; Nagell-Lutz implies P0 NON-TORSION. Explicit half Q=((W+E)(W+Y),(W+E)(W+Y)(E+Y)) is integral. Thus rank E4>=1 and rank Jac(C_EY)>=1 for EVERY primitive face slope. NEVER seek a uniform all-five-factor rank-zero proof, as that conclusion is FALSE.
SCOPE: This is NOT a no-Chabauty theorem (total rank<5 may hold for some fibers). Nor does E4-positive rank imply full physical positive endpoints: P0 has t=0 excluded, and real points must lift through three square roots plus original selectors and R<=B. No direct population factor, no uniform moving slope bound, no gate/credit/closure change.
REOPEN_CONDITION=A verified uniform rank<=4 + explicit C_EY rational-lift height bound with exact R-height and both moving-slope tails, or alternative selector-aware original high Gres physical summation. See card229EC; compare229EB and229DT-A1.
# K16-C3-M3 — NEGATIVE REGISTER
This file stores anti-loop cards and route-freeze explanations.
A negative result blocks only the stated mechanism. It is not a proof of impossibility outside its scope.
## NEG-CM-01 — fixed CM positive-rank filter
CARD:
M3-CM-NEG-001.
RESULT:
The fixed j=1728 quotient is already rank-positive before endpoint filtering.
There is a canonical rational section on the physical open, and it has infinite order generically.
CONSEQUENCE:
Stage14 rank-positive twist rarity does not distinguish endpoint from ordinary M3 points.
FROZEN IDEA:
Use rarity of positive rank in the fixed CM quotient as an endpoint filter.
REOPEN ONLY IF:
a genuinely different endpoint-specific quotient or rank condition is found.
## NEG-CM-02 — three local savings are dependent
CARD:
M3-CM-NEG-002.
RESULT:
In the CM/torsor route, base support plus the first rational point already implies the second-point local restriction prime-wise.
CONSEQUENCE:
The apparent three local restrictions cannot be multiplied as independent savings.
FROZEN IDEA:
cubic-in-support saving from three independent local conditions.
REOPEN ONLY IF:
a new condition is proved independent on the same exact physical measure.
## NEG-V4-01 — automorphisms do not mix endpoint state
CARD:
M3-V4-NEG-001.
RESULT:
The relevant quartic transformations form the generic V4 automorphism group and preserve endpoint squareclass on each orbit.
CONSEQUENCE:
Finite automorphism pairing cannot create a half-density endpoint contraction.
FROZEN IDEA:
pair V4 orbit points to force endpoint/non-endpoint mixing.
REOPEN ONLY IF:
a larger genuine correspondence acts nontrivially on endpoint state and has an exact population/height adapter.
## NEG-HEIGHT-01 — raw Master square boxes invalid
CARD:
M3-HEIGHT-NEG-001.
RESULT:
The exact physical region is product-shaped and may be strongly unbalanced.
Raw Master parameter square-box truncation is not an exact substitute for R<=B.
CONSEQUENCE:
Global counts must retain exact physical inequalities or prove a uniform adapter.
FROZEN IDEA:
derive global M3/endpoint exponents from a convenient raw square box with no physical comparison theorem.
REOPEN ONLY IF:
the proposed truncation is linked to exact R<=B by a proved lossless or summably lossy adapter.
## NEG-XG-01 — naive large-c multiplication invalid
CARD:
M3-XG-NEG-001.
RESULT:
In a balanced Euclid sector
r,s,m,n~T,
one has
R~T^4,
so R<=B gives T<=B^(1/4), and raw four-variable volume is ~B.
Therefore the c^2 lattice determinant acts on an ambient parameter family of scale ~B before endpoint/M3 restrictions are charged.
The known endpoint ceiling
P(B)<<B^(1/2+epsilon)
cannot simply be multiplied by c^-2.
CONSEQUENCE:
The heuristic
N_c(B)<<B^(1/2+o(1))c^-2
is not a theorem.
The tail
B^(1/2)/C
is not yet certified.
FROZEN IDEA:
discard large c solely by multiplying two savings proved on different measures.
REOPEN ONLY IF:
M3-XG-GATE-001 or another exact large-c theorem is proved.
## General anti-loop rules
A route remains frozen when its new proposal differs only by:
- a renamed coordinate;
- an algebraically equivalent squareclass;
- a different label for the same receiver;
- a local law with no same-measure transfer;
- a finite computation with no global theorem;
- multiplying savings whose independence is not proved.
A frozen route may reopen only when its card REOPEN_CONDITION is actually met.
NEGATIVE_REGISTER_STATUS=ACTIVE
## Amplified-u extreme Phi residual valuation — FROZEN / EMPTY
Canonical card:
M3-T11-043.
Frozen claim:
On every relative-fresh p^lambda||m_i with c_p=2*lambda,
v_p(Phi_*)=0.
More generally c_p>0 implies p does not divide Phi_*.
Reason:
The exact fourth-power denominator of the symmetric middle coefficient E forces p not dividing (b*s-a*t) on c_p=2*lambda.
Residual Phi_* divisibility would force the same Gaussian root orientation s==rho*t and therefore p|(b*s-a*t), contradiction.
Do not repeat:
- searches for v_p(Phi_*)>0 on c_p=2*lambda;
- attempts to extract extra valuation beyond the amplified-u extreme;
- multiplication of the E-denominator fact as an independent density saving.
Live remainder:
Only c_p=0, the full-S-denominator extreme, can carry residual fresh Phi_* valuation.
Its first normalized collision is
n_0*s == 16*a*b*t_0 mod p
after removing the forced p^(2*lambda) factor.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
Only if the exact E fourth-power denominator or exact common-u fresh saturation is retracted, or if a genuinely non-valuation same-measure mechanism is introduced.
## Fixed-E reciprocal second elliptic quotient — FROZEN
Canonical card:
M3-T11-044.
The physical fixed-E square lift is genus 3:
C_e: Z^2=-F_e(u^2).
Its canonical quotients are one elliptic factor E_e and one genus-2 factor H_e.
A Q-rational reciprocal commuting involution u->lambda/u would require
q0*q3^2-q4*q1^2=0.
For this family the obstruction is
4096*e^4*(e+4)^4*((e-6)^2+12),
which is nonzero for every nondegenerate rational e.
Do not repeat:
- generic reciprocal-involution searches on the fixed-E square lift;
- claims that physical x=u^2 automatically gives a second elliptic quotient;
- use of such a nonexistent quotient to replay the M3-T11-036 Dujella argument.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
An exact non-reciprocal elliptic factor of the genus-2 Prym with the required physical height/counting adapter, or a direct uniform genus-2/genus-3 theorem.
## Full-S-denominator naive Legendre tensorization — FROZEN AS FIXED-POWER ROUTE
Canonical card:
M3-T11-045.
On every c_p=0 fresh prime p^lambda||m_i, with n=2*lambda, the exact endpoint adapter gives
r^2==x*(1-x) mod p^n,
where
x=((1+u^2)S)/(16u).
This is a useful full-modulus same-measure router but not a fixed-power local sieve.
For p==1 mod 4, exactly (p-3)/2 nondegenerate residue classes x mod p make x(1-x) a nonzero square, and every such class has p^(n-1) lifts modulo p^n.
Thus the permitted local density is positive constant.
CRT over the c_p=0 conductor removes only a subpower factor of the form 2^(-omega(M_0))*M_0^o(1).
The singular carrier
x=-1/8
is worse for this local route:
x(1-x)=-9/64=-(3/8)^2,
and -1 is a square at every fresh split prime p==1 mod 4.
Hence the Legendre detector is automatically solvable there.
DO_NOT_REPEAT:
- do not claim fixed-power credit by multiplying the local quadratic-residue proportions over c_p=0 primes;
- do not count the Legendre detector as independent from the M3-T11-040 Q_S/C_u conductor allocation;
- do not discard x=-1/8 using the local detector.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
A same-physical-measure global dispersion/correlation theorem for realized x values, a genuine moving-x support theorem, or a separate counting theorem for the explicit singular carrier.

on fresh support.

On x=27/40, relative-fresh p=5 is impossible.
On x=-1/8, the conic c^2=a^2+2b^2 and the explicit three anti-roots force every fresh prime onto exactly one of the two factors c-b or c+b.
Up to permutation,
boxed:
one of m_1,m_2,m_3=1
identically on this carrier.
Do not reopen a three-nontrivial-fresh-core search there.
The resulting two-core Gaussian square-factor receiver places primitive conic parameters in determinant-M_rel^2 lattices after orientation/allocation is frozen.
This determinant is not fixed-power endpoint credit by itself.
A same-physical-measure R<=B range theorem is still required; the M3-T11-020 determinant/Jacobian firewall remains applicable against naive index charging.

## x=27/40 square-discriminant conic as a sufficient split criterion — FROZEN
On the x=27/40 singular carrier,
w^2=u^2-350
is exactly the discriminant-square condition for the anti-root cubic, but it is NOT sufficient for rational splitting.
Exact counterexample:
u=45/2,
w=25/2
lies on the conic. The cleared anti-root cubic is
4116841*z^3-1972188*z^2-16084980*z-7873200.
Modulo 11 it becomes
3*z^3+2*z^2+z+6,
which has no root in F_11, so the cubic is irreducible over Q.
M3-T11-047 gives the missing exact condition:
a Kummer cube in Q(sqrt(-3)), equivalently a rational point on the displayed x=27/40 genus-two curve outside the denominator-clearing boundary t=+/-1/3.
DO_NOT_REPEAT:
- do not identify square discriminant with full rational splitting;
- do not use the conic alone to count or eliminate the x=27/40 carrier;
- do not promote the bounded rational-point screen to theorem credit.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
Use the conic only together with the M3-T11-047 Kummer/genus-two condition, or replace both by a stronger exact full-splitting criterion.

## M3-T11-096 theta-branch residual-character / ordinary 2-isogeny routes — PROVISIONALLY FROZEN
Canonical dependency through M3-T11-095 remains audited; M3-T11-096 itself is provisional pending hostile audit.
On full-S, put M=M_rel, ell=M^2*L, kappa=(v+1)/M^2=N_0/b^2, Theta=theta/M^4. Then
16L+3kappa=M^2Theta,
so
L==-3kappa/16 mod M^2.
Together with W_flat^2=-N_0*Phi_flat this forces
boxed:
3L*Phi_flat==(3W_flat/(4b))^2 mod M^2.
Thus the M3-T11-092 residual-character square condition is automatic once the theta M^4 identity and normalized endpoint square are retained; it is not a standalone theta-branch saving.
For the translated fixed-theta elliptic curve
E_theta^-: Z^2=X(X^2+(9+4theta)X+4theta),
X=-(v+1),
the standard 2-isogeny sends the physical point to dual X-coordinate (3-2w)^2. The source Kummer class is [-(v+1)]=[-N_0], so the fresh M^2 conductor square disappears; the dual class is trivial. The associated two factors of 16theta are exactly the old M^2 factor from v+1 and the old M^2 theta cofactor.
DO_NOT_REPEAT pending audit:
- do not multiply the M3-T11-092 residual-character lock with theta M^4 as independent credit;
- do not use ordinary rational-2-torsion / 2-isogeny Kummer squareclasses on E_theta as a fresh-conductor detector;
- do not treat the square dual X-coordinate as a new physical square condition.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
Hostile audit rejects the M3-T11-096 algebra, or a new global theorem uses information strictly beyond these squareclass identities and proves a same-physical-measure moving-support/dispersion deficit.

## M3-T11-097 rational-2-torsion branch-depth / Kummer-iterate routes — PROVISIONALLY FROZEN
Canonical card:
M3-T11-097.
Retain audited M3-T11-096. On the theta!=0 full-S branch use U=-(v+1), A=v+1=-U and q=Z/A=3-2w on
E_theta^-: Z^2=U*(U^2+(9+4theta)U+4theta).
Then
A^2-(9+4theta-q^2)A+4theta=0,
and translation by T=(0,0) sends
U -> 4theta/U,
A -> 4theta/A,
q -> -q.
With A=M_rel^2*kappa and theta=M_rel^4*Theta, the translated branch also has exact M_rel^2 depth and
kappa*(4Theta/kappa)=4Theta.
Thus the two M_rel^2 factors are one 2-torsion exchange pair, not two independent support events.
Duplication satisfies
U(2P)=((U^2-4theta)/(2Z))^2,
so its apparent fourth-power depth is an exact square.
The ordinary T-Kummer class is
[U]=[-N_0],
hence multiplication-by-n cannot recover the forced M_rel^2 square factor.
After splitting the dual nonzero 2-torsion over Q(sqrt(theta))=Q(sqrt(Theta)), the displayed forced M_rel^2 remains a square factor in the two split Kummer coordinates. Residual bracket classes remain open and are not frozen.
DO_NOT_REPEAT pending hostile audit:
- do not count A and 4theta/A as independent M_rel^2 conditions;
- do not use the duplication square as a new fourth-power saving;
- do not iterate the ordinary rational-T Kummer map or multiplication-by-n as a fresh-conductor detector;
- do not claim split dual 2-descent saving solely from the forced M_rel^2 factor.
REOPEN_CONDITION:
Hostile audit rejects M3-T11-097, an audited dependency changes, or a genuinely new higher/non-Kummer descent uses the residual bracket classes or another invariant beyond the forced square factors to prove a same-physical-measure support deficit.

x=d*q^2,

The normalized residual quartic is
Q_u(z)=(1+u^2)z^4-8uz^3+(11u^2-1)z^2+6uz-u^2.
Its generic Galois group over Q(u) is S4: Q_u and the cubic resolvent are irreducible, while Disc(Q_u) has nonsquare class [2-25u^2].
For theta in the monic quartic algebra and every physical point,
N(z-theta)=-(T/(2^8*u^4*(1+u^2)))^2,
so the norm squareclass is always [-1].

## M3-T11-106 nonresonant interior S4 local squareclass route — PROVISIONALLY FROZEN
STATUS=PROVISIONAL / HOSTILE_AUDIT_REQUIRED / NO POWER SAVING.
On the exact physical image of audited M3-T11-105,
z=S/2.
For an interior fresh p^lambda||m_i, put A=1+u^2 and n=v_p(A)=2lambda+c_p. Translating the residual quartic by w=z-u/2 gives Newton lower vertices
(0,n),(3,0),(4,n),
hence one canonical Q_p-linear factor and one degree-3 factor.
If 3 does not divide n, the cubic factor is irreducible. Because it has odd degree 3 over the odd p-adic field, restriction on squareclasses is an isomorphism and norm is its inverse. The linear component has class [-8u/A], while audited total norm [-1] forces the cubic component to be res([A/(8u)]). Therefore the nonresonant interior prime carries no independent local non-norm squareclass beyond u/A.
If n=3m and A=p^n*a_0, the residual cubic after scaling is
xi^3-kappa_p,
kappa_p=27*a_0*u/128 mod p.
The only surviving primewise local branch is reducibility of this cubic:
- p==5 mod12: 1+2 split;
- p==1 mod12: split 1+1+1 iff kappa_p is a cube; otherwise irreducible and frozen by the odd-cubic collapse.
DO_NOT_REPEAT:

- do not seek independent primewise non-norm squareclass credit on nonresonant interior primes;

- do not infer global Selmer collapse or fixed-power saving.

## M3-T11-107 cubic-resonance local-squareclass route — PROVISIONALLY FROZEN
STATUS=PROVISIONAL / HOSTILE_AUDIT_REQUIRED / NO POWER SAVING.
At every interior fresh p, the finite degree-3 component of the physical M3-T11-106 quartic étale class is res([z]) componentwise, regardless of irreducible / 1+2 / 1+1+1 splitting. ([-8u/A],res([A/(8u)])).
